AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text="Build and tests pass.\n\n## Root cause\n\n`DocumentEditFragment.onResume()` unconditionally re-set the editor's text:\n\n```java\npublic void onResume() {\n    super.onResume();\n    checkReloadDisk();\n    _hlEditor.setText(_document.getContent());   // ← bug\n}\n```\n\n`TextView.setText()` discards the current selection and puts the cursor at offset 0. Since `onResume()` fires every time the app returns to the foreground — including when returning from another app, or from an activity/dialog like the insert-image picker — the cursor was reset to the start of the document on every resume. That also explains the secondary symptom: inserting an image after switching back put it at the beginning of the file, because the text-module action inserts at the (now zeroed) cursor.\n\nThe line was also redundant: `checkReloadDisk()` immediately above already reloads content via `loadDocumentIntoUi()` when the file genuinely changed on disk, and `loadDocumentIntoUi()` is the single funnel through which document content reaches the editor (verified — undo/redo and disk-reload are its only other callers).\n\n## Fix\n\nIn `app/src/main/java/net/gsantner/markor/activity/DocumentEditFragment.java`:\n\n1. **Removed the unconditional `setText` from `onResume()`.** The editor view survives a pause/resume, so it retains its own text and selection; only a real on-disk change needs a reload, which `checkReloadDisk()` still handles.\n\n2. **Hardened selection restore in `loadDocumentIntoUi()`.** Extracted a `setEditorSelection(start, end)` helper that clamps into the current text bounds and preserves the whole selection range rather than collapsing it. This also fixes a latent off-by-one in the old code, which clamped to `length() - 1` and so moved the cursor one character left of the end (and to `-1` on an empty document) whenever the position exceeded the new text length.\n\n3. **Persist the cursor across genuine view recreation.** Switching away from a big file can get the activity destroyed under memory pressure; the recreated `EditText` cannot know the old selection, so the position is now saved in `onSaveInstanceState` under `SAVESTATE_CURSOR_POS` and applied via a `_pendingCursorPos` field that takes precedence on the next `loadDocumentIntoUi()`.\n\nOne note on adjacent behavior I deliberately left alone: `DocumentIO.saveDocument()` decides whether to write by comparing against `getInitialVersion()` rather than the current content, so if a user edits text and then reverts it to exactly the original, nothing is written and `_document`/disk keep the intermediate value. That is a pre-existing save bug, out of scope here — but worth flagging that the old `onResume` line was actively making it worse, silently reverting the editor to the stale intermediate content. Since `onResume()` fires every time the app returns to the foreground — including when returning from another app, or from an activity/dialog like the insert-image picker — the cursor was reset to the start of the document on every resume. That also explains the secondary symptom: inserting an image after switching back put it at the beginning of the file, because the text-module action inserts at the (now zeroed) cursor.\n\nThe line was also redundant: `checkReloadDisk()` immediately above already reloads content via `loadDocumentIntoUi()` when the file genuinely changed on disk, and `loadDocumentIntoUi()` is the single funnel through which document content reaches the editor (verified — undo/redo and disk-reload are its only other callers).\n\n## Fix\n\nIn `app/src/main/java/net/gsantner/markor/activity/DocumentEditFragment.java`:\n\n1. **Removed the unconditional `setText` from `onResume()`.** The editor view survives a pause/resume, so it retains its own text and selection; only a real on-disk change needs a reload, which `checkReloadDisk()` still handles.\n\n2. **Hardened selection restore in `loadDocumentIntoUi()`.** Extracted a `setEditorSelection(start, end)` helper that clamps into the current text bounds and preserves the whole selection range rather than collapsing it. This also fixes a latent off-by-one in the old code, which clamped to `length() - 1` and so moved the cursor one character left of the end (and to `-1` on an empty document) whenever the position exceeded the new text length.\n\n3. **Persist the cursor across genuine view recreation.** Switching away from a big file can get the activity destroyed under memory pressure; the recreated `EditText` cannot know the old selection, so the position is now saved in `onSaveInstanceState` under `SAVESTATE_CURSOR_POS` and applied via a `_pendingCursorPos` field that takes precedence on the next `loadDocumentIntoUi()`.\n\nOne note on adjacent behavior I deliberately left alone: `DocumentIO.saveDocument()` decides whether to write by comparing against `getInitialVersion()` rather than the current content, so if a user edits text and then reverts it to exactly the original, nothing is written and `_document`/disk keep the intermediate value.
